'kuvāraṇṭaiṉ' etuvumiṉṟi naṭamāṭa aṉumatikkap peṟṟa vīrarkaḷuḷ inta mūvarkaḷē mutalāṉavarkaḷ āvar. ampuliyil mutaṉ mutalākak kārōṭṭiya appōlō - 15 viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷukku mūṉṟu kōṭi kārōṭṭikaḷ tam pārāṭṭaiyum makiḻcciyaiyum terivittaṉar. amerikka makkaḷ talaivar niksaṉ “appōlō - 15 payaṇam pūmiyiṉ varalāṟṟil oruputu yukattait tōṟṟuvikkakkūṭiya aṟiviyal aṟivai aḷikkum. mūṉṟu viṇveḷi vīrarkaṭkum nām talaivaṇaṅkukiṉṟōm“ eṉṟu pārāṭṭiṉār.

aṭiyiṟ kaṇṭavaṟṟai intap payaṇattiṉ uṟutip payaṉkaḷāka (rikārṭu cātaṉaikaḷāka'}k koḷḷalām:

1. appōlō-15 vīrarkaḷ ampuviyil irunta motta nēram 66 maṇi 55 nimiṭam. iḵtu appōlō-14 vīrarkaḷ irunta 33 maṇi 31 nimiṭa kālattaippōl iru maṭaṅkākum.

2. viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ ampulit taraiyil ārāyccikaḷ naṭattiya kālam 18 maṇi 37 nimiṭam; appōlō-14 vīrarkaḷ 9 maṇi 23 nimiṭa nēramē aṅṅaṉam kaḻittaṉar.

3. mutaṉ mutalāka ampulik kārai ōṭṭiyatu intap payaṇattiltāṉ. atu maṇikku 27.8 ki.mī. vītam ōṭṭap peṟṟatu.

4. viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ 171 irāttal ampulik kaṟkaḷaiyum maṇṇaiyum pūmikkuk koṇarntaṉar. itaṟku muṉṉar appōlō-14 vīrarkaḷ koṇarnta ivaṟṟiṉ eṭai 92.8 irāttalkaḷē.

5. ālḵpreṭ em. vōrṭaṉ eṉṟa kaṭṭaḷaip pakuti vīrar 20 nimiṭa nēram paranta vāṉa veḷiyil pūmiyiliruntu 313,600 ki.mī. uyarattil ulāp pōntār. pūmikkup puṟattiluḷḷa cuṟṟu veḷiyil mēṟkoṇṭa ulā ituvē mutalāvatākum.

6. ampuliyiṉ cuṟṟu vaḻiyil atikanēram taṅkiyatu (145 maṇi 15 nimiṭam) ituvē mutal muṟaiyākum.



7. vōrṭaṉ intap payaṇattil taṉiyāka 73 maṇinōm ampuliyaic cuṟṟiṉār. itukāṟum naṭaipeṟṟa ampulip payaṇaṅkaḷil cuṟṟiya nērattil ituvē atikanēram ākum.

8. appōlō ūrtiyiṉiṉṟum oru ceyaṟkait tuṇaikkōḷ ampuliyiṉ cuṟṟuvaṭṭattil iyakkiyatu ituvē mutal taṭavaiyākum.

intap payaṇattil kaṇṭa cila āyvumuṭivukaḷ ivai:

1. ampuliyiṉ tarai 25 ki.mī. āḻattukkuk keṭṭiyāka uḷḷatu eṉpatai uṟutiyākkiyatu.

2, ampuliyiṉ mēṭṭup pakutikaḷilum malaippakutikaḷilum aṅkuḷḷa paḷḷappakutikaḷiliruppataiviṭa alumiṉiyam atikamāka uḷḷatu.

3. ampuliyiṉ muṉpakkattaik kāṭṭilum piṉpakkattil keṭṭiyāṉa tarai 4 ki,mī. uyaram atikam iruppatu terintatu.

4. pūmiyil uḷḷataiviṭa ampuliyil mēl taraikkum aṭittaraikkum uḷḷa veppa ēṟṟattāḻvukaḷ atikam iruppatu uṟutiyāyiṟṟu.

5. ampuliyiṉiṉṟum koṇṭuvanta kaṟkaḷaiyum maṇṇaiyum ārāyvatāl mēlum pala takavalkaḷ terital kūṭum.





↑ iḵtu 1971 cūlai 25il toṭaṅki ākasṭu 7il niṟaivu peṟṟatu.

↑ appōlō-13 payaṇattil taṭai ēṟpaṭātiruppīṉ iḵtu 5āvatu payaṇamāka amaintirukkum.

↑ ākasṭu 9, 1971il





* * *





18. appōlō-16

intap payaṇam tiṭṭamiṭṭapaṭi 12 nāṭkaḷ 2 maṇi 36 nimiṭaṅkaḷ 36 vināṭikaḷ naṭaipeṟa vēṇṭiya payaṇamākum.[1] appōlō - 16 puṟappaṭṭa piṟaku atu cumār mūṉṟu maṇi nēram pūmiyai valam vanta piṟaku ampuliyai nōkkip puṟappaṭṭatu. atu maṇikku 39,200 ki. mī. vēkattil ceṉṟatu. pūmiyiliruntu atu puṟappaṭṭa pōtu ataṉ vēkam maṇikku 50,000 ki.mī. 36 māṭi uyaramuḷ eṉa cāṭṭarṉ - 5 eṉṟa irākkeṭṭu ataṉaic celuttiyatu.

intap payaṇattil kaṭaṟpaṭaiyaic cērnta kēpṭaṉ jāṉ ṭapilyū yaṅ (vayatu 41} vimāṉappaṭai lepṭiṉeṉṭ kāṉal cāres em. ṭiyūk vayatu 36), kaṭaṟpaṭai lepṭiṉeṉṭ kamāṇṭar tāmas kē māṭṭiṅkili (vayatu 35: paṅku koṇṭaṉar. intap payaṇattiṉ talaivar kēpṭaṉ yaṅ kuṭṭik kappalākiya ōriyaṉ ampuliyil iṟaṅkiya piṟaku māṭṭiṅkili tāyk kappalākiya kāsparil[2] iruntār; ampuliyai valamvantu koṇṭē ārāyccikaḷ naṭatti vantār. jāṉ yaṅkum cārlas ṭiyūkkum ampulittaraiyil iṟaṅki āyvukaḷ naṭattiṉar. ivarkaḷ cantiraṉil iṟaṅkiya oṉpatāvatu, pattāvatu amerikkarkaḷāvar. ivarkaḷuḷ jāṉ yaṅ 1965 mārccu mātam varjil ai kirisam eṉpavaruṭaṉ jemiṉi - 3 viṇveḷik kappalil ceṉṟavar. jemiṉi-10 payaṇattil talaimai vimāṉiyāka iruntavar. maṟṟaiya iruvarukkum[3] ituvē mutal viṇveḷip payaṇam ākum. āṉāl ampuliyiṉ 'ṭeskarēṭṭas' eṉṟa malaiyiṉ mītuḷḷa pīṭa pūmiyil mutaṉmuṟaiyāka iṟaṅkiyavarkaḷ eṉṟa vakaiyil ivarkaḷatu cātaṉai viṇveḷi āyvu varalāṟṟil oru taḷicciṟappup peṟukiṉṟatu.

intap payaṇattil pala aṟiviyal cōtaṉaikaḷ mēṟ koḷḷappeṟṟaṉa. ac cōtaṉaikaḷukkāṉa karuvikaḷ viṇveri vīrarkaḷ tirumpiya piṟakum aṅkēyē viṭṭuviṭap peṟṟaṉa. avai terivikkum ceytikaḷ vāṉoli alaikaḷ mūlam pūmikku vantu koṇṭirukkum. cila cōtaṉaikaḷ appōlō - 11 muyaṟciyōṭu toṭaṅkiyavaiyē. ippayāṇattil ampulit taraiyil naṭattappeṟṟa cōtaṉaikaḷ varumāṟu :

1. puvisi ; iḵtu ampuli alṭrāvayalaṭ kāmirā! spekṭrō kirāḵp (yuvisi) pukaippaṭak karuviyum tolai tōkkiyum iṇaintatu. viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ mutalil pūmiyaip paṭam piṭippar. pūmiyiṉ mītuḷḷa vaḷi maṇṭalattai ārāya iḵtu utavum. aṭuttu, ampulit taraiyiṉ toṭuvāṉattai nōkkip paṭam eṭuppar. erimalai vāyu ētāvatu anta iṭattiliruntāl ippaṭattil tulaṅkum. ampulit taraiyiliruntu vāṉiyal paṭaṅkaḷ eṭukkap peṟuvatu ituvē mutal muṟaiyākum.

2 veppam paravutal cōtaṉai (ec. eḵp. i): ampulit taraiyil iraṇṭu tuḷaikaḷai iṭṭu ampuliyiṉ uṭpuṟattiliruntu veppam veḷiyēṟum aḷaviṉai aḷantaṟiyum cōtaṉai itu.

3. iyaṟkai nila atirvuc cōtaṉai (pi. es. i): pūmiyil tila atirccikaḷai aḷappataṟku utavum karuvi pōṉṟa oru karuviyiṉāl ampulit taraiyil iyaṟkaiyāka ēṟpaṭum mikamikac ciṟiya atirvukaḷum ic cōtaṉaiyāl aḷantaṟiyap peṟum.

4. ceyaṟkai tila atirvuc cōtaṉai (ē. es. . i) ampulit taraikku aṭiyiluḷḷa amaippai ārāyvataṟkāka aṅkuc ceyaṟkaiyākac cila atirvukaḷ uṇṭupaṇṇap peṟum. anta atirvukaḷiṉ viḷaivu oṉṟukkoṉṟu 46 mīṭṭar tolaivil vaikkappeṟum iraṇṭu uṇarvuk karuvikaḷāl aḷaviṭappeṟum.

5. nilāttarai kāttamaṇṭala āyvu {el, es. em.) : eṭuttuc cellakkūṭiya kāntamāṉiyiṉ utaviyāl, pala ilakkukaḷil ampuliyiṉ kānta maṇṭalam aḷantaṟiyap peṟum.

6. katiravak kāṟṟu āyvu : - katiravaṉiṭamiruntu mikamika nuṇṇiya tukaḷkaḷ perumpālum iṭaiviṭāmal puṟattē eṟiyap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivai katirava maṇṭalam muḻuvatum paravukiṉṟaṉa. intap paraval nilaiyaittāṉ katiravak kāṟṟu eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. ik kāṟṟiṉ vēkam vināṭikkuc cila kilōmīṭṭar tolaivākum. viṇveḷi vimāṉikaḷ it tukaḷkaḷaip piṭittup pūmikkuk koṇarvar.

7. aṇṭakkatir āyvu : aṇṭakkatirkaḷ (Cosmic rays) eṉpavaiyum kiṭṭattaṭṭa oḷiyiṉ vēkattil (vināṭikku 2,97,600 ki.mīṭṭarkaḷ ) pāyum nuṇtukaḷkaḷē perumpālum ivai purōṭṭāṉ, ālḵpāt tukaḷkaḷākum. aṇṭak katirkaḷ katirava maṇṭalattiṟkup puṟattiliruntē varukiṉṟaṉa; āṉāl, avai eṅkiruntu varukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu viḷaṅkā viyaṉulakap putirākavē uḷḷatu. ivaṟṟai ampulit taraiyil kaṇṭaṟiyac cōtaṉaikaḷ mēṟkoḷḷap peṟum.

8. ampuliyiṉ nila uṭkūṟu āyvu : viṇveḷi vimāṉikaḷ tāṅkaḷ ceṉṟu iṟaṅkum 'ṭeskarēṭṭas' eṉṟa pakutiyil nila uṭkūṟu eṅṅaṉam uḷatu eṉpataṉai āyntu aṅkuḷḷa maṇ, kal mutaliyavaṟṟil vakaikku oṉṟiraṇṭu eṭuttu varuvar.

9. ampuli maṇ āyvu : ampuli maṇṇiṉ iyaṅku iyalpukaḷai ārāyat taraiyil amiḻntu tāṉē pativu ceytukoḷḷum karuvi oṉṟiṉaip payaṉpaṭuttuvar.

appōlō - 16 viṇveḷik kappaliṉ eṭṭukkāl pūcci vaṭivuḷḷa 'ōriyaṉ' eṉṟa ampuli ūrti mutalil tiṭṭamiṭṭataṟku 5 maṇi 42 nimiṭam tāmatamāka ampuliyiṉ malaippakutiyil pātukāppāka iṟaṅkiyatu. inta ūrtiyiḷ mūlamāka maṉitaṉ aintāvatu muṟaiyāka ampuliyil kālaṭi vaikkiṉṟāṉ. appōlō - 16 kappal 16āvatu muṟaiyākac cantiraṉai valam vantu koṇṭirunta poḻutu jāṉ yaṅkum cārlas ṭiyūkkum iṟaṅkat toṭaṅkiṉar. 'ōriyaṉ' ampuliyiṉ mēṟparappait toṭṭatum ataṉ cāmpal niṟat tūcu paṟantatu ampuli ūrtiyil ceṉṟavarkaḷ cōtaṉaikaḷai muṭittut tirumpum varaiyilum 'kāspar' eṉṟa tāykkappal ampulikku 106 kilō mīṭṭar tolaivil vaṭṭamiṭṭuk koṇṭiruntatu tāmas māṭṭiṅkili eṉṟa viṇveḷi vīraruṭaṉ.

jāṉ yaṅkum cārlas ṭiyūkkum ampuliyil 71 maṇi tēram taṅkiṉar. inta nērattil mūṉṟu muṟai tam ūrtiyai viṭṭu veḷic ceṉṟu āyvukaḷai naṭattiṉar. avarkaḷ cumār 10 kilō mīṭṭar tūram ampuliyil ceṉṟaṉar. malaiyil 250 mutal 300 mīṭṭar tūram ēṟiṉar. malaiyaṭivārattil kāṭupōl irunta pāṟaikaḷ mītum, malaiccarivu mītum avarkaḷ jīp ceṉṟatu; pala taṭavai atu kutittuk kutittuc ceṉṟatu. avarkaḷ ampuli jīppai oṭṭakam pōl ōṭṭic ceṉṟaṉar. karaṭumuraṭāṉa payaṇattiṉ poḻutu carivu aḷakkum karuviyaiyum, piḷpuṟa ṭeṇṭaraiyum, piṉpuṟac cakkarat tiruppum karuviyaiyum, iṟutiyil perumpālum ōṭṭuc cātaṉam muḻuvataiyum jīp iḻantu viṭṭatu. oru kaṭṭattil avarkaḷ tam jīppai maṇikku 17 kilō mīṭṭar vēkattil ōṭṭiṉar. jīp payaṇam 27•1 ki.mī, taṭaipeṟṟatu.

ampuli vīrarkaḷ ūrtikku veḷiyē 20 maṇi 14 nimiṭam iruntu āyvukaḷ naṭattiṉar, mūṉṟu kaṭṭac cōtaṉaikaḷum veṟṟiyuṭaṉ naṭantēṟiṉa. mūṉṟāvatu ampuli ulāviṉ poḻutu vaṭakku rē {North Ray) eṉṟa āḻamāṉa ampulikkuḻi oṉṟaik kaṇṭaṉar. ik kuḻi nilā ūrti iṟaṅkiyuḷḷa iṭattiṉiṉṟum 5-1 ki.mī. vaṭa ticaiyil uḷḷatu. kuḻiyiṉ viṉimpil vaḻuvaḻuppāṉa kal vaḷaiyam uḷḷatu. itukāṟum ampuliyil maṉitaṉ eṭṭip pārttirāta ak kuḻi 341 ki.mī. nīḷaviṭṭamum 180 mīṭṭar āḻamum uḷḷatākum eṉṟu oḷippaṭam mūlam āyntu matippiṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. niṉaippiṟkum eṭṭāta neṭuṅkālattiṟku mūṉṉar oru viṇkal cantiraṉilirunta ōr erimalaik kuḻappil mōtiyatāl intak kuḻi ēṟpaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu karutappeṟukiṉṟatu. inta iṭattil kiṭanta kaṟkaḷil cila cumār 9 mīṭṭariliruntu 14 mīṭṭar nīṉam uḷḷavaiyāka iruntaṉa. ampuliyiṉ tōṟṟattaip paṟṟiyum ataṉ pū iyal paṟṟiyum aṟivataṟku inta iṭattil cēkarikkappeṟum kaṟkaḷ peritum utavak kūṭum eṉṟu aṟiviyalaṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar.

mēṟkaṇṭa iṭattil tēvaiyāṉa aḷavu palvēṟu vitak kaṟkaḷaic cēkarittuk koṇṭu ampuli vīrarkaḷ taṅkaḷ jīppil 'pukaimalai' eṉṟa iṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟaṉar. iḵtu erimalaik kuḻampu uṟaintataṉ kāraṇamāka 400 kōṭi yāṇṭukaṭku muṉ uṇṭāṉa malaiyāka irukkum eṉṟu karutap peṟukiṉṟatu.

ampulic cōtaṉaikaḷai muṭittuk koṇṭu jāṉ yaṅkum cārlas ṭiyūkkum ampuli ūrtiyil ēṟit tāyk kappalai aṭaintaṉar. ataṉ piṟaku māṭṭiṅkili iraṇṭu paṇikaḷai niṟaivēṟṟiṉār.

mutalāvatu: 7.5 mīṭṭar nīḷamuḷḷa kayiṟṟāl tāyk kappaluṭaṉ piṇaikkap peṟṟirunta avar tāyk kappaliṉ piṉpuṟattil kāmirākkaḷ vaittirunta pakutikkuc ceṉṟu antak kāmirākkaḷilirunta ḵpilim curuḷkaḷai eṭuttu vantu tāykkappalukkuḷ koṇṭuvantu cērttār. ampuliyiṉ palvēṟu pakutikaḷaip paṭam piṭittu vaittirunta antac curuḷiṉ nīḷam cumār 16 ki.mī. irukkum. pūmiyiliruntu pārttāl teriyāta ampuliyiṉ piṉpuṟap pakutiyaiyum anta ḵpilimil pārkkalām.

iraṇṭāvatu : māṭṭiṅkili - niṟaivēṟṟiya aṭutta paṇi itu. cumār āṟu kōṭi nuṇṇaṇukkirumikaḷ (Backtria) aṭaṅkiya ōr uṟaiyait tiṟantu ataṉmītu cūriyaṉiṉ katir iyakkam paṭiyumpaṭi kāṇpittār. pakalavaṉiṉ katirvīcciṉāl inta uyirkaḷ evvāṟu pātikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉpataik kaṇṭaṟivutē itaṉ nōkkam. inta iru paṇikaḷum niṟaivēṟa 62 nimiṭaṅkaḷ āyiṉa.

māṭṭiṅkiliyiṉ viṇveḷi ulā tolaikkāṭciyil kāṭṭap peṟṟatu. itukāṟum ampuli ūrtiyil ceṉṟa yaṅkum ṭiyūkkum maṭṭilumē kāṭciyaḷittaṉar. māṭṭiṅkili antarattil "nīntiya“ kāṭciyait tolaikkāṭciyil kāṇa avar tuṇaiviyār (eṭṭut tiṅkaḷ cūl nirampiyavar) talaimai nilaiyattiṟku vantiruntār. viṇveḷik kappaliliruntu māṭṭiṅkili veḷi vantapōtu tolaikkāṭciyil mutalil avaratu kālkaḷ terintaṉa. piṉṉar. avaratu uṭal terittatu. vēkamākac ceṉṟu koṇṭirunta tāykkappaluṭaṉ kūṭavē avar talaikīḻākap pōyk koṇṭiruppatu terintatu. avarukkup piṉṉāl avaraip piṇaittirunta kayiṟum, tāykkappaliṉ cāḷaram oṉṟiṉ mūlam avaraiyē nōkkik koṇṭirunta maṟṟa iru viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷiṉ mukaṅkaḷum tolaikkāṭcip paṭattiṉ oru mūlaiyil terintaṉa.

appōlō-16 iṉ tāyk kappal mūṉṟu viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷuṭaṉ pacipik mākaṭalil hōṉalulu tīvukkut teṟkē 2400 ki. mī. tolaivil iṟaṅkiyatu. inta iṭattiliruntu 1. 6 ki. mī. tolaivil mīṭpuk kappal kāttiruntatu. tāyk kappal kaṭalil iṟaṅkiya kāṭciyait tolaikkāṭciyil kaṇṭukaḷittaṉar pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa makkaḷ.

inta appōlō - 16 iṉ payaṇam 44.5 kōṭi ṭālar (311. 5 kōṭi rūpāy) celavil mēṟkoḷḷap peṟṟatu. payaṇam ceyta nēram 266 maṇi (11 nāḷ - 2 maṇi) payaṇam ceyta tūram 22,25,600 ki. mī. tāykkappal pūmiyaic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa vaḷi maṇṭalattiṉ vaḻiyāka vanta pōtu ataṉ vēkam maṇikku 39,699 ki.mī. (24,183 mail). viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ ampuliyiṉiṉṟum cēkarittuvanta, nilāk kaṟkaḷiṉ eṭai 110 kilō kirām. itaṉuḷ 18kilō eṭaiyuḷḷa oru periya pāṟaiyum aṭaṅkum.

viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ ampuliyil viṭṭuvanta 'ōriyaṉ' ampuliyai 343 nāṭkaḷ vaṭṭamiṭṭu varum. iṟutiyil ampuliyil vīḻntu noṟuṅkum. itaṉait tavira ivarkaḷ viṭṭuviṭṭu vanta āyvuk kōḷkaḷ 92 nāṭkaḷ ampuliyai vaṭṭamiṭṭu varum.

inta varicaip payaṇaṅkaḷil iṉṉum orē oru payaṇam tāṉ uḷḷatu. atil mutal muṟaiyākap pautika iyal vallunar oruvar aṉuppi vaikkappeṟuvār.





↑ iḵtu 1972 ēpral 16.il toṭaṅki ēpral 28 ik niṟaivu peṟṟatu.

↑ tāykkappalukkuk kāspar eṉṟum ampuliyil iṟaṅkum ūrtikku ōriyaṉ eṉṟum peyariṭṭaṉar. 'kāspā' eṉpatu kēlic cittirak kataikaḷil aṭikkaṭi varum oru paṭakkataip pāttiram: tiraippaṭam, tolaikkāṭcikaḷilum tōṉṟum 'kāspā' maṟṟavarkaṭku nalam ceyyum oru kuṟaḷi, 'ōriyaṉ' eṉpatu miruka cīriṭa naṭcattirak kūṭṭam. kirēkkap purāṇak kataippaṭi, ōriyaṉ aḻa tam valimaiyum paṭaitta oru periya vēṭaṉ. iṭuppil ciṅkattōlum mūṉṟu naṭcattiraṅkaḷ uḷḷa kaccaiyum aṇintavaṉ, taṭiyum vāḷum tarittavaṉ. appōlō - 16 viṇveḷi vimāṉikaḷ ampuli ceṉṟu varum payaṇam muḻuvatilum mirucucīriṭam avarkaṭkut teriyum; ataik koṇṭu avarkaḷ ticai. kaṇṭu koḷḷalām. (aṭutta pakkam pārkka)

(muṉ pakkat toṭarcci)

appōlō - 16 il mūvarum payaṇam ceykaiyil ṭeksṭās mānilattil hūsṭaṉ nakariluḷḷa viṇveḷip payaṇak kaṇkāṇippu nilaiyattār. vīṇveḷi vimāṉikaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpu. koḷḷuṅkāl 'appōlō-16' eṉṟu aḻaippar. nilāvūrti taṉiyākap pirintavuṭaṉ 'kāspar' eṉṟum, 'ōriyaṉ' eṉṟum kūppiṭuvar. ikkāraṇattāl tāṉ ivaṟṟiṟkut taṉippeyarkaḷ iṭappeṟṟaṉa

↑ ivarkaḷuḷ kaṭṭiṅkili eṉpār appōlō - 18. payaṇattil jermaṉ taṭṭa kālammai tākkutaliṉ kāraṇamāka aṉuppap peṟavillai eṉpatum, avarukkup patilāka cuvikārṭ eṉpavar aṉuppap peṟṟār eṉpatum niṉaivu kūrat takkavai.





* * *





19. appōlō - 17

appōlō - 17 viṇveḷip payaṇam[1] ulakiṟku 45āvatu viṇveḷip payaṇamākum ; amerikkāviṟku 27 āvatu payaṇamākum. ampuliyaic cuṟṟi amerikka viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ ceṉṟa oṉpatāvatu payaṇam itu. ampuliyaic cuṟṟi vanta payaṇaṅkaḷilē mūṉṟu payaṇaṅkaḷ nīṅkalāka maṟṟellāp payaṇaṅkaḷilum viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ ampuliyil iṟaṅkiṉar. 1968 ṭicamparil appōlō - 8 viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ ampuliyai mutaṉ mutalākac cuṟṟiṉar. 1969 cūlai mātam appōlō-11 vīrarkaḷ mutaṉmutalāka ampuviyil aṭiyeṭuttu vaittaṉar. piṉṉar tikaḻnta appōlō 13 payaṇam nīṅkalāka maṟṟellā appōlōp payaṇaṅkaḷilum viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ ampuliyil iṟaṅkiṉar. viṇveḷik kalattil ēṟpaṭṭa kaṭumaiyāṉa cīrkēṭu kāraṇamāka appōlō -13 viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ ampuliyil iṟaṅka muṭiyavillai. avarkaḷ ampuliyai valam vantu tirumpi viṭṭaṉar. appōlō-17 payaṇattuṭaṉ maṉitaṉ. aṅku iṟaṅkuvatu āṟāvatu taṭavaiyākum. pattu āṇṭukaṭku muṉpiruntu āyattamāṉa amerikka ampulip payaṇat tiṭṭattiṟku muttāyppu vaittatupōl amainta payaṇam itu.

ampulip payaṇaṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum mika nīṇṭatu inta appōlō - 17 iṉ payaṇam.[2] itil yūjīṉ ē. cerṉāṉ (Eugene A. Cernan) (vayatu 40) rāṉalṭu i. ivāṉs (Ronald E. Evans) (vayatu 39) haricaṉ ec. skimit (Harrison H. Schmitt) (vayatu 37) ākiya mūvarum paṅkēṟṟaṉar. ivarkaḷuḷ cerṉāṉ talaimai vimāṉi; ivāṉs 'amerikkā' eṉṟa tāykkappaliṉ vimāṉi; skimit 'cālañcar' (Challenger) eṉṟa nilākkalattiṉ vimāṉi. cerṉāṉ 1966 il viṇveḷi vimāṉiyāṉavar. itaṟkumuṉ irumuṟai viṇveḷip payaṇam ceytavar ivar. 1966 cūṉ mātam jemiṉi - 9 viṇveḷik kalattil ceṉṟapōtu kalattiṟku veḷiyil vantu viṇveḷiyil 2 maṇi 10 nimiṭam naṭantu kāṭṭiyavar ; viṇveḷik kalam oṉṟu pūmiyai oru muḻuc cuṟṟu cuṟṟum varaiyil ākāyattil naṭanta mutal maṉitar. piṉṉar 1969 il mē mātam appōlō 10 viṇveḷik kalattil ampuliyai 31 muṟai valam vantu ampulit, taraikkup patiṉaintu kilō mīṭṭar uyaram varai ceṉṟu tirumpiyavar ivar. āka mottam ituvarai patiṉoru nāṭkaḷukku mēl viṇveḷiyil payaṇam ceyta vīrarāvār. intap payaṇattil ivāṉs tāykkappalil taṉiyē ampuliyaic cuṟṟikkoṇṭu āyvukaḷ naṭattiṉār. itaṟku muṉṉar ivar viṇveḷi ceṉṟatillai. cerṉāṉ, skimit ākiya iruvarum ampulit taraiyil iṟaṅki āyvukaḷ taṭattiṉar. ivarkaḷ muṟaiyē nilāt taraiyil iṟaṅkiya 11-vatu, 12-vatu maṉitarkaḷāvar. skimit oru nila uṭkūṟṟiyal aṟiviyalaṟiñar; ṭākṭar paṭṭam peṟṟavar. ivar oru māṇi (Bachelor). aṟiviyalaṟiñar oruvar, mutaṉ mutalāka ampulikkuc ceṉṟavar ivarē yāvār. pūmiyil pāṟaikaḷaiyum pāṟaikaḷiṉ amaippaiyum ārāyvatil ivar vallunar, ampuliyiṉ pāṟai āyvukkākavē ivar 1965 il viṇveḷi vimāṉiyākat tērnteṭukkap peṟṟavar.

ituvē iravil puṟappaṭṭa mutaṟ payaṇam ākum. iravil ēṉ puṟappaṭavēṇṭum ? ampuliyil nammai nōkkiyirukkum pakkattil ēṟattāḻa viḷimpiṟkum maiyattiṟkum naṭuvil vaṭakiḻakkup pakutiyil ṭāras-liṭrāv (Taurus-Littrow)[3] eṉṟa malaippāṅkāṉa iṭattil iṟaṅkavēṇṭum, ataṟkuk keṉṉaṭi muṉaiyiliruntu kiḷampiṉāl iravil tāṉ puṟappaṭa vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu. appoḻutu tāṉ vaḻiyil eri poruḷ celavu kuṟaivāka irukkum. iṭaiyil kalam cellum vaḻiyaic caṟṟu māṟṟit tiruttavēṇṭiya vēlaiyum kuṟaiyum. iṟaṅkum iṭattilum iṟaṅkum nērattil katiravaṉ vacatiyāṉa uyarattiliruntu oḷi vīcum.

tērnteṭukkap peṟṟa 'ṭāras - liṭrāv' eṉṟa ilakkut taṉicciṟappuṭaiyatu. itu vēṟupaṭṭa iyaṟkait tōṟṟam uṭaiyatu ; nila uṭkūṟṟiyalpaṟṟiya pala vakai amaippukaḷ koṇṭatu. iṅkuḷḷa kuṉṟup pakutikaḷai mūṭiyirukkum veḷir niṟap poruḷ ampuli tōṉṟiya kālattaiyoṭṭiyatu, eṉa. aṟiviyalaṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar. cumār 2,100 mīṭṭar uyaramuḷḷa ampulimalaiyiṉ arukilirukkum paḷḷattākkiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷum, malaiyiṉ mītum paḷḷattākkukaḷilum cila karumporuḷkaḷum erimalaikaḷiṉiṉṟum uruki vaḻintu piṉpu iṟukiyavai eṉṟu karutappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivai ampuliyiṉ - uṭpuṟattil mika āḻattiṉiṉṟum ōraḷavukku aṇmaik kālattil veḷippaṭṭavaiyāka iruttalvēṇṭum.

katirava maṇṭalam, pirapañcam ivai tōṉṟiya vitam paṟṟi ārāyum aṟiviyalaṟiñarkaṭkup paḻaimaiyāṉa poruḷkaḷ perumakiḻcci tarum. cumār 450 kōṭi āṇṭukaṭku muṉpu ampuli tōṉṟiyatāka matippiṭukiṉṟaṉar avarkaḷ. vāyu mēkaṅkaḷ iṟuki aḵtu uṇṭāṉatāka oru karuttu nilavukiṉṟatu. ampulip poruḷkaḷiṉ mātirikaḷ appōtirunta nilaimaipaṟṟit tupput tulakkak kūṭum. erimalaik kuḻampu mātirikaḷil kōḷiyal aṟiñarkaṭkup pērararvam uṇṭu. ivarkaḷ pūmiyum ampuliyum uṭpaṭap palvēṟu kōḷkaḷum evvāṟu tōṉṟiṉa eṉṟu āyvukaḷai mēṟkoḷpavarkaḷ. ampuli tōṉṟiya kālam mutal iṉṟuvarai aḵtu ettakaiya māṟṟaṅkaḷai aṭaittatu eṉpatai aṟiya ipporuḷkaḷ tuṉai ceyyum.

mēṟkuṟippiṭṭa 'ṭārs-liṭrāv' eṉṟa ilakku 'amaitik kaṭal' (Sea of Serenity) eṉṉum ampulip pakutiyai oṭṭi amaintuḷḷatu. aṇmaiyil naṭaipeṟṟa ampuli āyvukaḷiṉ mūlam ippakuti oru 'māskāṉ' (MassConcentration) eṉak kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. aḵtāvatu, ip pakuti mikunta poruṇmaic ceṟivu uḷḷatākat teriya vantuḷḷatu. iṅku ulōkat taṉimaṅkaḷ irukkak kūṭum. it taṉimaṅkaḷ ampuliyaic cuṟṟivarum ceyaṟkaik kalaṅkaḷiṉ vaṭṭap pātaiyaip pātikkum vakaiyil īrppu vicaiyaic celuttupavai eṉṟu karutap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. appōlō - 17 inta ilakkukku mēlē paṟantu ceṉṟapōtu 'māskāṉkaḷ' paṟṟiyum, avai ceyaṟkaik kalaṅkaḷ mītu uṇṭākkum viḷaivukaḷ paṟṟiyum tuṇukkamāṉa āyvukaḷ naṭattappeṟṟaṉ.

cerṉāṉum skimittum miṉvicai mōṭṭār vaṇṭiyil ampulit taraiyil mūṉṟu muṟai cuṟṟi vantaṉar. appōtu avarkaḷ ceyta mukkiyamāṉa paṇikaḷuḷ oṉṟu nilāt taraiyil āyvu nilaiyam amaittatākum. inta āyvu nilaiyam palavakaiyāṉa karuvit tokutikaḷaik koṇṭatu. ik karuvikaḷ tāmākavē iyaṅkupavai ; tammil pativākum takavalkaḷait tāmē neṭu nāṭkaḷukku oliparappip pūmikku aṉuppik koṇṭirukkum. itaṟku - muṉṉarc ceṉṟa aintu appōlō kalaṅkaḷum niṟuvivanta aintu āyvu nilaiyaṅkaḷum aṟiviyal takavalkaḷai iṉṟum aṉuppik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.



muṉṉaiya appōlō kalaṅkaḷ eṭuttuc ceṉṟa karuvikaḷ tavirap putitākap pattukkaruvikaḷaip palvēṟu cōtaṉaikaḷai mēṟkoḷḷum poruṭṭu appōlō - 17 ampuli maṇṭalattiṟku eṭuttuc ceṉṟatu. avaṟṟuḷ ēḻu, nilāt taraiyil naṭattum ārāyccit tokuppaic cārntavai. oṉṟu, pūmi mutaliya viṇkōḷkaḷ ampuliyaik kavarntu īrkkum vicaiyiṉ taṉmaiyai ārāyntu kūṟum. maṟṟoṉṟu, ampuliyiṉ uḷḷiruntu ciṟuciṟu aḷavukaḷil tappittu veḷivarum vāyukkaḷiṉ mūlakkūṟukaḷai ārāyavallatu. piṟitoṉṟu, viṇveḷiyiṉiṉṟum vantu ampulit taraiyil paṭiyum tūciyiṉ aḷavaik kaṇikkavum viṇkaṟkaḷāl tākkuṇṭu peyarntu viḻum nilāttaraip poruḷkaḷāl uṇṭākum nila arippiṉai aḷaviṭavum vallatu. iṉṉoṉṟu, ampuliyiṉ pautika iyalpukaḷai āyvataṟkāka veṭikuṇṭukaḷai veṭittu ārāyum taṉmaiyuṭaiyatu.

nilāt taraiyiṉ aṭiyil pāṟaikaḷ eṉṉa eṉṉa vakaiyil aṭuk kaṭukkāka uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu mēṟparappiṉ miṉ vicait taṉmaikaḷaikkoṇṭu ārāyum karuvi aintāvatu. itaṉ payaṉāka ampuliyiṉ uḷḷē nīr uḷḷatā eṉpataik kaṇṭaṟiyalām. ampuliyiṉ īrppuvicai evvāṟu paraviyuḷḷatu eṉpatai āṟāvatu karuvi āyum. itaṉai viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ nilā ūrtiyil vaittu atil ēṟiccellum iṭaṅkaḷilellām payaṉpaṭutti īrppuvicai nilappaṭam varaiya utavuvar. 'nilā 'niyūṭrāṉ āyvu' eṉṟa ēḻāvatu karuvi nilāttaraiyil ēṟpaṭum arippukaḷ paṟṟiya ceytikaḷait tayārittut tarum. ampulittaraiyil iṟaṅkiya iruvarum intak karuvikaḷaik kaiyāṇṭu āyvukaḷai naṭattiṉar. ivarkaḷ ampuliyil mūṉṟu nāḷ mūṉṟu maṇi oru nimiṭanēram taṅkiyiruntaṉar. iḵtu appōlō - 16 viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ aṅkut taṅkiyirunta nērattaiviṭa, nāṉku maṇinēram atikamākum.



"amerikkā“ eṉṟa tāyk kalattil nilaviṉai valam vantukoṇṭirunta ivāṉs maṟṟa mūṉṟu karuvi kaḷaiyum kaiyāṇṭu āyvukaḷ naṭattiṉār. ivar paṇip pakutiyil uḷḷa inta mūṉṟu āyvukkaruvikaḷaiyum oḷippaṭak karuvikaḷaiyum iyakki vaittār. oru karuvi ampulit taraikku 13 kilō mīṭṭar āḻattiluḷḷa nilaimaikaḷai "rāṭār alaikaḷ“ koṇṭu ārāyum taṉmaiyatu. maṟrueru karuvi ampuliyiṉ iruṇṭa maṟupakkattiṉ amaippiṉai uṟṟaṟivataṟkāka aṅkuḷḷa mēṟparappiṉ veppa nilai iṭattukku iṭam vēṟupaṭuvataik kaṇṭaṟiya vallatu. iṉṉoru āyvukkaruvi viṇveḷikkalam veḷiyiṭum pukai mutaliya vāyukkaḷ eppaṭi eppaṭip paravukiṉṟaṉa ? avai ampuliyiṉ arukē evvaḷavu nēram taṅkiyirukkiṉṟaṉa ? eṉpavaṟṟait terivikkum.

inta nīṇṭa payaṇattil viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ mūvarum tiṭṭamiṭṭapaṭi ellāc cōtaṉaikaḷaiyum veṟṟikaramāka muṭittaṉar. intap payaṇattiṉ putumaikaḷil oṉṟu nilavil iṟaṅkiya viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ ampulit taraiyil kaṇṭa ārañcu niṟap paḷḷattākku ākum. iṅkuḷḷa maṇṇai iruvarum cēkarittaṉar. ittakaiya maṇṇai evarum kaṇṭatillai. ampuliyil tōṉṟum erimalaiyiṉāl veḷippaṭum kuḻampil ulōkap poruḷkaḷ irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu karutappeṟukiṉṟatu. ap poruḷkaḷ uyiriyattuṭaṉ (Oxygen) cērntu inta ārañcu niṟa maṇ (Rust) uṇṭākiyirukkavēṇṭum. eṉṟu ūkam ceykiṉṟaṉar. iḵtu uṇmaiyāyiṉ ampuliyil nīrum uyiriyamum iruntirukkavēṇṭum eṉṟum, itiliruntu ampulik kaṭalkaḷil 300 kōṭi āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar erimalaik kuḻampu nirampiyirunta poḻutu aḵtu uyiraṟṟup pōyviṭavillai eṉpatu terikiṉṟatu eṉṟum karutukiṉṟaṉar.



inta appōlō payaṇattil aintu ciṟiya cuṇṭelikaḷum[4] viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷuṭaṉ payaṇam ceytaṉa. maṉitarum piṟa uyirkaḷum ampulikkup payaṇam ceytatu ituvē mutal taṭavaiyākum. viṇveḷiyil aṇṭak katirkaḷ (Cosmicrays) paraviyuḷḷaṉa. avai maṉitarait tākkum poḻutu palvēṟu uṟuppukaḷilum palavakai viḷaivukaḷai uṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉa. ik katirkaḷ mūḷaiyil ettakaiya viḷaivukaḷai uṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatai aṟiya intac cuṇṭelikaḷ ampuli maṇṭalattiṟku eṭuttuc cellap peṟṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ mūḷaiyil aṇṭak katirkaḷaip pativuceyyum karuvikaḷ poruttap peṟṟiruntaṉa. appōlōk kalattil ivāṉs inta elikaḷuṭaṉ ampuliyaic cuṟṟik koṇṭiruntār. pūmikkut tirumpiya piṉṉar ivvelikaḷiṉ mūḷaiyai ārāyntu atil aṇṭak katirkaḷiṉ viḷaivukaḷaik kaṇittu aṟivar.



↑ itu ṭicampar 7ām nāḷ (1972) toṭaṅki 19-ām nāḷ niṟaivu peṟṟatu.

↑ puṟappaṭṭatiliruntu vantu iṟaṅkum varai āṉa kālam 12nāḷ 16 maṇi 31 nimiṭam ākum. appōlō-15 viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ taṅkiya nērattai viṭa maṇi nēram atikamākum.

↑ turukki nāṭṭiluḷḷa pāras eṉṟa malaittoṭariṉ peyaraiyum 19 āvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻnta āstiriya nāṭṭu vāṉiyal vallunarum kaṇita mētaiyumāṉa viṭrāv eṉpār peyaraiyum iṇaittu iṭappeṟṟatu ip peyar.

↑ viraliṉ nuṉi aḷavu uḷḷavai. ovvoṉṟiṉ eṭaiyum 1/3 avuṉs aḷavē yākum, maṉitarkaṭku muṉṉar viṇveḷikkuc ceṉṟa piṟa uyirkaṭkup peyar vaittatu pōl ivaṟṟiṟkup peyar vaikka villai.





* * *





20. etirkālat tiṭṭaṅkaḷ

aṭutta irupattaintu āṇṭukaḷ varaiyil amerikkā putitāka nilavup payaṇam etaiyum mēṟkoḷḷap pōvatillai eṉṟu aṟivittuḷḷatu. iraṣyāvum ampulikku maṉitaṉai aṉuppum ārāycciyai mēṟkoḷvatāka aṟivikkavillai. eṉavē, inta nūṟṟāṇṭil ampuliyil maṉitaṉ ārāycciyum appōlō-17 iṉ payaṇattuṭaṉ muṭivuṟṟatākak koḷḷalām. āyiṉum, ituvarai ampuliyil iṟaṅkiya āṟu appōlō kalaṅkaḷum[1] niṟuvi vantuḷḷa āṟu āyvu nilaiyaṅkaḷum toṭarntu aṟiviyal ceytikaḷai aṉuppikkoṇṭē irukkum. ākavē, iṉṉum nīṇṭa nāṭkaḷukku ampuli āyvu ippuviyil toṭarntu naṭai peṟṟuk koṇṭirukkum. mēlum, ampuliyiṉiṉṟum ippuvikkuk koṇarappeṟṟa poruḷkaḷai āyntu aṟiviyal uṇmaikaḷaik kāṇum paṇiyum eḷitil muṭiyum eṉṟu colluvataṟkillai. eṉavē ampulip payaṇam niṉṟuviṭṭālum, ampuli paṟṟiya putupputuc ceytikaḷ toṭarntu veḷiyākikkoṇṭē irukkum eṉpatai nām etirpārkkalām.

itaṟkiṭaiyil amerikkā putumaiyāṉa iru perun tiṭṭaṅkaḷai mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷatu. avaiyāvaṉa :

(1) viṇveḷi - āyvakam - (sky lab) : - iḵtu āṟu aṟaikaḷ koṇṭa oru vīṭu pōṉṟu peritāka irukkum. itu viṇveḷiyil celuttappeṟum.[2] itil maṉitar evarum cellār. āṉāl, maṟu nāḷ appōlō kalam, oṉṟil mūṉṟu viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ paṟantu ceṉṟu pūmiyai valam vantu koṇṭirukkum viṇveḷi āyvuk kūṭattil iṇaivar. piṉṉar avarkaḷ āyvakattil nuḻaintu aṅku amaikkappeṟṟirukkum karuvikaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttic cōtaṉaikaḷ naṭattuvar. 28 nāṭkaḷ kaḻittut tam kalattiṉuḷ mīṇṭum pukuntu pūmikkut tirumpuvar, viṇveḷi āyvakam kāliyākap pūmiyaic cuṟṟik koṇṭirukkum. iraṇṭu tiṅkaṭkup piṉṉar iraṇṭāvatu viṇveḷi vīrarkaḷ kuḻu puviyiṉiṉṟum puṟappaṭṭu āyvakattai aṭaiyum. aṅku ivarkaḷ 55 nāṭkaḷ taṅki āyvukaḷ naṭattip pūmikkut tirumpuvar. oru māta iṭaiveḷikkup piṉṉar mūṉṟāvatu kuḻu ceṉṟu āyvakattil iṉṉoru 56 nāṭkaḷ taṅki āyvukaḷai mēṟkoḷḷum. iḵtu amerikkāviṉ viṇveḷi āyvakam paṟṟiya tiṭṭam ākum.

mēṟkūṟiya ittiṭṭam puvi āyvai maiyamākak koṇṭatu. naṭappu āṇṭil (1973) eṭṭut tiṅkaḷ kālam viṇveḷi āyvakam pūmiyaic cuṟṟi valamvantu koṇṭirukkum. inta āyvakam tiraṭṭiya paṭaṅkaḷaiyum ceytikaḷaiyum āyntu pūmipaṟṟiya ciṟappāṉa ceytikaḷait tayārikka intiyā uṭpaṭa 17 nāṭukaḷaic cērnta aṟiviyalaṟiñarkaḷai 'nācā' eṉṟa viṇveḷi nilaiyam tērnteṭuttuḷḷatu. ivarkaḷuṭaiya āyvu vēḷāṇmai, kāṭu vaḷarppu, mīṉ piṭippu, cūḻ nilaikkēṭu taṭuppu, kaṉivaḷam kāṇṭal, vaṟaṭci, veḷḷat taṭuppu, nilap paṭam varaital pōṉṟa palvēṟu tuṟaikaḷukkup peritum utavum.

inta āyvakam viṇveḷi āyvukaḷ naṭattavum payaṉpaṭum. pūmiyaic cūḻntu kāṟṟu maṇṭalam uḷḷatāl pūmiyiṉiṉṟum viṇveḷi āyvukaḷai mēṟkoḷḷum poḻutu iṭaiyilirukkum vaḷimaṇṭalam aṟiviyal uṇmaikaḷait taṭuttu viṭukiṉṟatu ; cila camayam tirittum viṭukiṉṟatu. vaḷimaṇṭalattaik kaṭantu viṇ veḷiyil cuḻalum āyvakattiliruntu āyvukaḷai mēṟkoḷḷum poḻutu inta iṭaiyūṟukaḷ akalum. ātaliṉ, inta muṟaiyil putiya ceytikaḷ tulliyamākak kiṭaikkum vāyppu uṇṭākiṉṟatu.

(2) viṇveḷi - ōṭam (Space shuttle) : viṇveḷi āyvakat tiṭṭattai aṭutta muyaṟci itupaṟṟiyatu. iḵtu 1978il mēṟkoḷḷap peṟum. viṇveḷiyil puviyaic cuṟṟip paṟantu koṇṭirukkum viṇveḷi āyvakam allatu viṇveḷi araṅkiṟkup (Space plateform) pūmiyiliruntu ceṉṟuvarap payaṉpaṭuvatu viṇveḷi ōṭam. itu viṇ veḷikkup paṟantu ceṉṟu, taṉatu paṇi muṭinta piṉṉar, puvikkut tirumpi oru cātāraṇa vimāṉam iṟaṅkuvatu pōṉṟu pātukāppāka vimāṉa taḷattil iṟaṅka vallatu. itē ōṭattai mīṇṭum mīṇṭum 100 muṟai payaṉpaṭuttalām. itaṉāl viṇveḷip payaṇac celavu kuṟaikiṉṟatu. 12 payaṇikaḷaiyum 4 vimāṉikaḷaiyum koṇṭu cella valla itil evarum payaṇam ceyyalām. appōlō kalattai iyakkic cellum viṇveḷi vimāṉikaḷukku irukkavēṇṭiya aḷavu tiṟamai inta ōṭattai iyakkum vimāṉikaṭku irukka vēṇṭiyatillai. ivarkaḷuṭaiya tiṟam, cōtaṉai vimāṉikaṭkum (Test pilots) viṇveḷi vimāṉikaṭkum (Astronauts) iṭaippaṭṭatāka iruntāl pōtum eṉṟu kaṇikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu. āyiṉ, ivarkaḷ kaṭaṟpayaṇat tuṟaiyil nalla payiṟci peṟṟavarkaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum.

kaṭal maṭṭattiliruntu cumār 1200 mīṭṭarukkum atikamāṉa uyarattiliruntu viṇveḷi ōṭattaic celuttuvatu ukantatāka irukkum eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉai viṇveḷikku untic cellum irākkeṭṭukaḷ itaṉ veḷippuṟamāka amaikkap peṟṟirukkum. viṇveḷi ōṭam mēlē ceṉṟu pūmiyaic cuṟṟat toṭaṅkiyatum inta irākkeṭṭukaḷ tāmākak kaḻaṉṟu pūmiyai vantaṭaiyum. ivaṟṟai mīṇṭum payaṉpaṭuttalām. pūmiyiṉ cuṟṟuvaḻiyil ōṭivarum ōṭam aṅku mitantu varum viṇveḷi āyvakattuṭaṉ iṇaiyum. appoḻutu ōṭattil uḷḷavarkaḷ āyvakattiṉuḷ nuḻaintu koḷvar. piṉṉar inta ōṭam pirintu pūmiyai nōkki vantu vimāṉa taḷattil pātukāppāka iṟaṅkum. inta ōṭattai mitavaik kuṭai (Parachute) vāyilākavum kaṭalil iṟaṅkac ceyyalām. ippoḻutu mēṟkoḷḷappeṟum viṇveḷip payaṇattil oru viṇveḷik kalattaip pūmiyaic cuṟṟi valamvarumāṟu aṉuppa ōr irāttal eṭaikkuc cumār 600 mutal 700 ṭālar (oru ṭālar 7 rūpāy) varai celavākiṟa tākak kaṇakkiṭṭuḷḷaṉar; āṉāl, viṇveḷi ōṭattaiyum ataṉaic celuttum irākkeṭṭukaḷaiyum {Liquid Rockets) paṉmuṟai payaṉ paṭuttalāmātaliṉ, inta muṟaiyil ōr irāttal eṭaikkuc cumār 100 ṭālarukkuk kuṟaivākavē celavākum eṉak kaṇakkiṭṭuḷḷaṉar. 1980-varai intat tiṭṭaṅkaḷ ceyaṟpaṭṭu varum.

intap pattāṇṭuk kālattil ip puviyōriṉ karuttiṉaik kavarum vēṟoru ceytiyum uḷḷatu, aḵtāvatu, 1975 il amerikkāviṉ appōlō kalamum iraṣyāviṉ cōyūs kalamum viṇṇilē iṇaiyap pōkiṉṟaṉa. kōṭpāṭukaḷil etirmuṉaiyil uḷḷa inta iru nāṭukaḷum viṇveḷit tuṟaiyai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu aṟiviyal toḻil nuṭpat tuṟaikaḷil taṉittaṉiyē kaṇṭuvarum muṉṉēṟṟaṅkaḷiṉ parimāṟṟattiṟku itaṉāl oruvaḻiyum tōṉṟalām. intak kūṭṭu muyaṟciyāl viṇveḷittuṟaiyiluḷḷa cila cikkalkaḷum tīrkkappeṟalām. itaṉāl amerikka viṇveḷi vimāṉikaḷ tam takutikaḷuṭaṉ iraṣyamoḻi kaṟkavēṇṭiya nilaiyum tōṉṟalām.





↑ ivaṟṟuḷ mūṉṟu ampuliyīṉ vaṭapātiyilum mūṉṟu ataṉ teṉpātiyilumāka amaintuḷḷaṉa. inta āṟil iraṇṭu mēṟpātiyilum, nāṉku kīḻppātiyilum irukkum.

↑ 1973 - ēpral 30 ām nāḷ.





* * *





inta miṉṉūlaip paṟṟi


uṅkaḷukku immiṉṉūl, iṇaiya nūlakamāṉa, vikkimūlattil iruntu kiṭaittuḷḷatu[1].

inta iṇaiya nūlakam taṉṉārvalarkaḷāl vaḷarukiṟatu. vikkimūlam patiya taṉṉārvalarkaḷai varavēṟkiṟatu. tāṅkaḷum vikkimūlattil iṇaintu mēlum pala miṉṉūlkaḷai aṉaivarum paṭikkumāṟu ceyyalām.

mikunta akkaṟaiyuṭaṉ meyppu ceytālum, miṉṉūlil piḻai ētēṉum iruntāl tayakkam illāmal, vikkimūlattil immiṉṉūliṉ pēccu pakkattil terivikkalām allatu piḻaikaḷai nīṅkaḷē kūṭa cari ceyyalām.

ippaṭaippākkam, kaṭṭaṟṟa urimaṅkaḷōṭu (potukaḷa /kuṉu -Commons /GNU FDL )[2][3] ilavacamāka aḷikkappaṭukiṟatu. eṉavē, inta uraiyai nīṅkaḷ maṟṟavarōṭu pakiralām; māṟṟi mēmpaṭuttalām; vaṇika nōkkattōṭum, vaṇika nōkkamiṉṟiyum payaṉpaṭuttalām

immiṉṉūl cāttiyamāvataṟku paṅkaḷittavarkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu:

Deepa arul kaniyam

Rabiyathul kaniyam

Arularasan. G

Balajijagadesh

Sridhar G

Rabiyathul

TI Buhari

Info-farmer

Joshua-timothy-J

HoboJones

Fleshgrinder

Be..anyone

Xato

Mecredis
